          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_1} G inst._@.Kourovka.Problems.P21_40.Statement.688641198._hygCtx._hyg.3))))

    @LT.lt.{0} (@Fin n) (@instLTFin n) j i → @Eq.{1} @Rat (A i j) (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} @Rat 0 (@Rat.instOfNat 0)))
